- **Wilson loop**: Square contour, edges $z_1, z_2, z_3, z_4$.
- **Eikonal vertex**: $(i g v_i^{\mu_k} t^{a_k})$ on edge $i$.
- **Gluon propagator**: $\frac{-i g_{\mu\nu} \delta^{ab}}{\ell^2 + i0}$
- **Dimension**: $D = 4 - 2\varepsilon$, Feynman gauge.
